# Anthropic's Fable 5.1 Hits 60.9% on Humanity's Last Exam, GPT-6 Astra Drops, & the Cybercab Takeover

## Creator description

The mates sit down with Emad Mostaque to discuss: GPT-6 Astra’s ARC-AGI-3 performance, Anthropic’s Fable 5.1 frontier results, Tesla’s $30K Cybercab takeover, the collapse of mobility costs, AI cyber risks, and the growing debate over banning artificial superintelligence.
